     1	"""Storage abstraction and in-memory implementation for knowledge discovery.
     2	
     3	Follows design.md §3:
     4	- Store interface defining contracts for agents, profiles, and messages.
     5	- InMemoryStore providing a standalone, testable implementation without Firestore dependency.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
    11	from copy import deepcopy
    12	import threading
    13	from typing import Any
    14	
    15	from knowledge_discovery.models import (
    16	    Agent,
    17	    Card,
    18	    MailSeed,
    19	    Message,
    20	    Profile,
    21	    Schedule,
    22	    Task,
    23	    utc_now_iso,
    24	)
    25	
    26	
    27	class Store(ABC):
    28	    """Abstract storage interface for knowledge discovery entities."""
    29	
    30	    # Agent operations (agents collection / registry)
    31	    @abstractmethod
    32	    def save_agent(self, agent: Agent) -> None:
    33	        """Save or update an agent record in the registry."""
    34	        pass
    35	
    36	    @abstractmethod
    37	    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    38	        """Retrieve an agent by agent_id."""
    39	        pass
    40	
    41	    @abstractmethod
    42	    def get_agent_by_employee_id(self, employee_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    43	        """Retrieve an agent by employee_id."""
    44	        pass
    45	
    46	    @abstractmethod
    47	    def list_agents(self, active_only: bool = False) -> list[Agent]:
    48	        """List registered agents, optionally filtering for active only."""
    49	        pass
    50	
    51	    # Profile operations (profiles collection)
    52	    @abstractmethod
    53	    def save_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> None:
    54	        """Save or update an employee knowledge profile."""
    55	        pass
    56	
    57	    @abstractmethod
    58	    def get_profile(self, employee_id: str) -> Profile | None:
    59	        """Retrieve a profile by employee_id."""
    60	        pass
    61	
    62	    @abstractmethod
    63	    def list_profiles(self) -> list[Profile]:
    64	        """List all employee profiles."""
    65	        pass
    66	
    67	    # Message operations (messages collection / audit log)
    68	    @abstractmethod
    69	    def save_message(self, message: Message) -> None:
    70	        """Save or update a message record."""
    71	        pass
    72	
    73	    @abstractmethod
    74	    def get_message(self, audit_id: str) -> Message | None:
    75	        """Retrieve a message by audit_id."""
    76	        pass
    77	
    78	    @abstractmethod
    79	    def list_messages(self, limit: int | None = None) -> list[Message]:
    80	        """List all messages in chronological order."""
    81	        pass
    82	
    83	    @abstractmethod
    84	    def get_messages_for_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> list[Message]:
    85	        """Retrieve messages involving the given entity (as sender, recipient, or participant)."""
    86	        pass
    87	
    88	    # Task operations (tasks collection §14.2)
    89	    @abstractmethod
    90	    def save_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
    91	        """Save or update a task record."""
    92	        pass
    93	
    94	    @abstractmethod
    95	    def list_tasks(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Task]:
    96	        """List tasks, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""
    97	        pass
    98	
    99	    # Schedule operations (schedules collection §14.2)
   100	    @abstractmethod
   101	    def save_schedule(self, schedule: Schedule) -> None:
   102	        """Save or update a schedule reminder."""
   103	        pass
   104	
   105	    @abstractmethod
   106	    def list_schedules(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Schedule]:
   107	        """List schedules, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""
   108	        pass
   109	
   110	    # MailSeed operations (mail_seeds collection §14.2, §14.5)
   111	    @abstractmethod
   112	    def save_mail_seed(self, mail: MailSeed) -> None:
   113	        """Save or update an email seed record."""
   114	        pass
   115	
   116	    @abstractmethod
   117	    def get_mail_seed(self, mail_id: str) -> MailSeed | None:
   118	        """Retrieve an email seed by mail_id."""
   119	        pass
   120	
   121	    @abstractmethod
   122	    def list_mail_seeds(
   123	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, unprocessed_only: bool = False
   124	    ) -> list[MailSeed]:
   125	        """List mail seeds, optionally filtered by owner and/or unprocessed status."""
   126	        pass
   127	
   128	    # Card operations (cards collection §14.2)
   129	    @abstractmethod
   130	    def save_card(self, card: Card) -> None:
   131	        """Save or update a secretary card."""
   132	        pass
   133	
   134	    @abstractmethod
   135	    def get_card(self, card_id: str) -> Card | None:
   136	        """Retrieve a card by card_id."""
   137	        pass
   138	
   139	    @abstractmethod
   140	    def list_cards(
   141	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, status: str | None = None
   142	    ) -> list[Card]:
   143	        """List cards, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id and/or status."""
   144	        pass
   145	
   146	    @abstractmethod
   147	    def find_open_card_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> Card | None:
   148	        """Find an open stagnation card for the given task and owner."""
   149	        pass
   150	
   151	    @abstractmethod
   152	    def find_cards_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> list[Card]:
   153	        """Find all stagnation cards for the given task and owner."""
   154	        pass
   155	
   156	    @abstractmethod
   157	    def try_confirm_card(self, card_id: str) -> tuple[Card | None, bool]:
   158	        """Atomically transition a card's status from 'open' to 'confirmed' (CAS, §14.4).
   159	
   160	        Returns (card, won):
   161	        - (None, False) if no card with this card_id exists.
   162	        - (card, True) if THIS call performed the open->confirmed transition; the
   163	          returned card reflects the post-transition ('confirmed') state.
   164	        - (card, False) if the card exists but was not in 'open' status (already
   165	          'confirmed' by a concurrent caller, or 'dismissed'/'resolved'/'applied');
   166	          the returned card reflects its current stored state.
   167	
   168	        Implementations must guarantee at most one caller ever observes won=True
   169	        for a given card_id (Firestore: @firestore.transactional; in-memory: a
   170	        lock guarding the read-check-write).
   171	        """
   172	        pass
   173	
   174	    @abstractmethod
   175	    def clear(self) -> None:
   176	        """Clear all stored data (useful for test isolation)."""
   177	        pass
   178	
   179	
   180	class InMemoryStore(Store):
   181	    """In-memory implementation of Store using Python data structures."""
   182	
   183	    def __init__(self) -> None:
   184	        self._agents: dict[str, Agent] = {}
   185	        self._profiles: dict[str, Profile] = {}
   186	        self._messages: list[Message] = []
   187	        self._messages_by_id: dict[str, Message] = {}
   188	        self._tasks: dict[str, Task] = {}
   189	        self._schedules: dict[str, Schedule] = {}
   190	        self._mail_seeds: dict[str, MailSeed] = {}
   191	        self._cards: dict[str, Card] = {}
   192	        self._card_lock = threading.Lock()
   193	
   194	    def save_agent(self, agent: Agent) -> None:
   195	        """Save or update an agent record in the registry."""
   196	        stored = deepcopy(agent)
   197	        self._agents[stored.agent_id] = stored
   198	
   199	    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> Agent | None:
   200	        """Retrieve an agent by agent_id."""
   201	        agent = self._agents.get(agent_id)
   202	        return deepcopy(agent) if agent is not None else None
   203	
   204	    def get_agent_by_employee_id(self, employee_id: str) -> Agent | None:
   205	        """Retrieve an agent by employee_id."""
   206	        for agent in self._agents.values():
   207	            if agent.employee_id == employee_id:
   208	                return deepcopy(agent)
   209	        return None
   210	
   211	    def list_agents(self, active_only: bool = False) -> list[Agent]:
   212	        """List registered agents, optionally filtering for active only."""
   213	        agents = list(self._agents.values())
   214	        if active_only:
   215	            agents = [a for a in agents if a.active]
   216	        return [deepcopy(a) for a in agents]
   217	
   218	    def save_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> None:
   219	        """Save or update an employee knowledge profile."""
   220	        stored = deepcopy(profile)
   221	        self._profiles[stored.employee_id] = stored
   222	
   223	    def get_profile(self, employee_id: str) -> Profile | None:
   224	        """Retrieve a profile by employee_id."""
   225	        profile = self._profiles.get(employee_id)
   226	        return deepcopy(profile) if profile is not None else None
   227	
   228	    def list_profiles(self) -> list[Profile]:
   229	        """List all employee profiles."""
   230	        return [deepcopy(p) for p in self._profiles.values()]
   231	
   232	    def save_message(self, message: Message) -> None:
   233	        """Save or update a message record."""
   234	        stored = deepcopy(message)
   235	        if stored.audit_id in self._messages_by_id:
   236	            for idx, existing in enumerate(self._messages):
   237	                if existing.audit_id == stored.audit_id:
   238	                    self._messages[idx] = stored
   239	                    break
   240	        else:
   241	            self._messages.append(stored)
   242	        self._messages_by_id[stored.audit_id] = stored
   243	
   244	    def get_message(self, audit_id: str) -> Message | None:
   245	        """Retrieve a message by audit_id."""
   246	        msg = self._messages_by_id.get(audit_id)
   247	        return deepcopy(msg) if msg is not None else None
   248	
   249	    def list_messages(self, limit: int | None = None) -> list[Message]:
   250	        """List all messages in chronological order."""
   251	        messages = [deepcopy(m) for m in self._messages]
   252	        if limit is not None and limit > 0:
   253	            return messages[-limit:]
   254	        return messages
   255	
   256	    def get_messages_for_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> list[Message]:
   257	        """Retrieve messages involving the given entity (as sender, recipient, or participant)."""
   258	        results: list[Message] = []
   259	        for msg in self._messages:
   260	            participants = msg.payload.get("participants", []) if isinstance(msg.payload, dict) else []
   261	            if (
   262	                msg.from_entity == entity_id
   263	                or msg.to_entity == entity_id
   264	                or entity_id in participants
   265	            ):
   266	                results.append(deepcopy(msg))
   267	        return results
   268	
   269	    # Task operations
   270	    def save_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
   271	        stored = deepcopy(task)
   272	        self._tasks[stored.task_id] = stored
   273	
   274	    def list_tasks(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Task]:
   275	        tasks = list(self._tasks.values())
   276	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   277	            tasks = [t for t in tasks if t.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
   278	        return [deepcopy(t) for t in tasks]
   279	
   280	    # Schedule operations
   281	    def save_schedule(self, schedule: Schedule) -> None:
   282	        stored = deepcopy(schedule)
   283	        self._schedules[stored.item_id] = stored
   284	
   285	    def list_schedules(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Schedule]:
   286	        schedules = list(self._schedules.values())
   287	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   288	            schedules = [s for s in schedules if s.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
   289	        return [deepcopy(s) for s in schedules]
   290	
   291	    # MailSeed operations
   292	    def save_mail_seed(self, mail: MailSeed) -> None:
   293	        stored = deepcopy(mail)
   294	        self._mail_seeds[stored.mail_id] = stored
   295	
   296	    def get_mail_seed(self, mail_id: str) -> MailSeed | None:
   297	        m = self._mail_seeds.get(mail_id)
   298	        return deepcopy(m) if m is not None else None
   299	
   300	    def list_mail_seeds(
   301	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, unprocessed_only: bool = False
   302	    ) -> list[MailSeed]:
   303	        mails = list(self._mail_seeds.values())
   304	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   305	            mails = [m for m in mails if m.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
   306	        if unprocessed_only:
   307	            mails = [m for m in mails if not m.processed]
   308	        return [deepcopy(m) for m in mails]
   309	
   310	    # Card operations
   311	    def save_card(self, card: Card) -> None:
   312	        stored = deepcopy(card)
   313	        self._cards[stored.card_id] = stored
   314	
   315	    def get_card(self, card_id: str) -> Card | None:
   316	        c = self._cards.get(card_id)
   317	        return deepcopy(c) if c is not None else None
   318	
   319	    def list_cards(
   320	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, status: str | None = None
   321	    ) -> list[Card]:
   322	        cards = list(self._cards.values())
   323	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   324	            cards = [c for c in cards if c.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
   325	        if status is not None:
   326	            cards = [c for c in cards if c.status == status]
   327	        return [deepcopy(c) for c in cards]
   328	
   329	    def find_open_card_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> Card | None:
   330	        for c in self._cards.values():
   331	            if (
   332	                c.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id
   333	                and c.type == "stagnation"
   334	                and c.status == "open"
   335	                and c.payload.get("task_id") == task_id
   336	            ):
   337	                return deepcopy(c)
   338	        return None
   339	
   340	    def find_cards_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> list[Card]:
   341	        results: list[Card] = []
   342	        for c in self._cards.values():
   343	            if (
   344	                c.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id
   345	                and c.type == "stagnation"
   346	                and c.payload.get("task_id") == task_id
   347	            ):
   348	                results.append(deepcopy(c))
   349	        return results
   350	
   351	    def try_confirm_card(self, card_id: str) -> tuple[Card | None, bool]:
   352	        """Atomically transition a card's status from 'open' to 'confirmed' (CAS)."""
   353	        with self._card_lock:
   354	            card = self._cards.get(card_id)
   355	            if card is None:
   356	                return None, False
   357	            if card.status != "open":
   358	                return deepcopy(card), False
   359	            card.status = "confirmed"
   360	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   361	            self._cards[card.card_id] = deepcopy(card)
   362	            return deepcopy(card), True
   363	
   364	    def clear(self) -> None:
   365	        """Clear all stored data."""
   366	        self._agents.clear()
   367	        self._profiles.clear()
   368	        self._messages.clear()
   369	        self._messages_by_id.clear()
   370	        self._tasks.clear()
   371	        self._schedules.clear()
   372	        self._mail_seeds.clear()
   373	        self._cards.clear()

     1	"""Proactive Secretary Layer (Milestone 3, Phase A).
     2	
     3	Follows design.md §14:
     4	- Deterministic stagnation score with 5 signals & 2-tier thresholds (T1/T2, FR17-18).
     5	- Assert T1 < T2 at startup.
     6	- Sweep task state machine: notice -> request_draft promotion, done -> resolved, dismissed non-recreation (FR16).
     7	- Public-only preview search using embedding_public without side effects (FR19).
     8	- Atomic confirmation CAS routing to existing query flow (FR20).
     9	- Morning digest dynamically aggregating schedule reminders and open cards (FR22).
    10	- Proactive profile diff proposal from mail seeds and 4-way review (FR23).

    43	W_RESCHED: float = float(os.environ.get("W_RESCHED", "2.0"))
    44	W_NEGLECT: float = float(os.environ.get("W_NEGLECT", "3.0"))
    45	W_UNTOUCHED: float = float(os.environ.get("W_UNTOUCHED", "2.0"))
    46	
    47	STAGNATION_T1: float = float(os.environ.get("STAGNATION_T1", "3.0"))
    48	STAGNATION_T2: float = float(os.environ.get("STAGNATION_T2", "7.0"))
    49	STAGNATION_CAP: int = int(os.environ.get("STAGNATION_CAP", "10"))
    50	STAGNATION_NEGLECT_WINDOW: int = int(os.environ.get("STAGNATION_NEGLECT_WINDOW", "3"))

    56	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    57	# Date Helpers (§14.7)
    58	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59	
    60	def get_today(demo_today: str | None = None) -> date:
    61	    """Return the base date for stagnation and schedule calculations.
    62	
    63	    Uses DEMO_TODAY environment variable (or argument) if set, otherwise UTC date.
    64	    """
    65	    raw = demo_today or os.environ.get("DEMO_TODAY")
    66	    if raw:
    67	        try:
    68	            # Handle ISO date (YYYY-MM-DD) or timestamp
    69	            if "T" in raw:
    70	                return datetime.fromisoformat(raw.replace("Z", "+00:00")).date()
    71	            return date.fromisoformat(raw.strip())
    72	        except (ValueError, TypeError):
    73	            pass
    74	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).date()
    75	
    76	
    77	def _parse_date_or_timestamp(val: str | None) -> date | None:
    78	    """Safely parse a date or ISO timestamp string into a date object."""
    79	    if not val or not str(val).strip():
    80	        return None
    81	    val_str = str(val).strip()
    82	    try:
    83	        if "T" in val_str:
    84	            return datetime.fromisoformat(val_str.replace("Z", "+00:00")).date()
    85	        return date.fromisoformat(val_str)
    86	    except (ValueError, TypeError):
    87	        return None
    88	
    89	
    90	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    91	# Stagnation Score Calculation (§14.3)
    92	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    93	
    94	def calculate_stagnation_score(
    95	    task: Task,
    96	    all_owner_tasks: list[Task],
    97	    today: date | None = None,
    98	    w_overdue: float = W_OVERDUE,
    99	    w_stale: float = W_STALE,
   100	    w_resched: float = W_RESCHED,
   101	    w_neglect: float = W_NEGLECT,
   102	    w_untouched: float = W_UNTOUCHED,
   103	    cap: int = STAGNATION_CAP,
   104	    neglect_window: int = STAGNATION_NEGLECT_WINDOW,
   105	) -> tuple[float, str, dict[str, Any]]:
   106	    """Compute rule-based stagnation score and synthesized evidence line.
   107	
   108	    Signals:
   109	    1. Overdue days: days past due date (capped).
   110	    2. Stale days: days since last update (capped).
   111	    3. Reschedule count: count of reschedules.
   112	    4. Relative neglect: task stale >= neglect_window while another task of owner updated within neglect_window.
   113	    5. Untouched: status == 'todo' and created_at == status_changed_at.
   114	
   115	    Returns:
   116	        (score, evidence_line, signals_dict)
   117	    """
   118	    ref_today = today or get_today()
   119	
   120	    # 1. Overdue days
   121	    due_d = _parse_date_or_timestamp(task.due_date)
   122	    overdue_days = max(0, (ref_today - due_d).days) if due_d is not None else 0
   123	    capped_overdue = min(overdue_days, cap)
   124	
   125	    # 2. Stale days
   126	    upd_d = _parse_date_or_timestamp(task.last_updated_at) or _parse_date_or_timestamp(task.created_at)
   127	    stale_days = max(0, (ref_today - upd_d).days) if upd_d is not None else 0
   128	    capped_stale = min(stale_days, cap)
   129	
   130	    # 3. Reschedule count
   131	    resched_count = max(0, task.reschedule_count)
   132	
   133	    # 4. Relative neglect
   134	    relative_neglect = 0
   135	    if stale_days >= neglect_window:
   136	        for other in all_owner_tasks:
   137	            if other.task_id == task.task_id:
   138	                continue
   139	            other_upd_d = _parse_date_or_timestamp(other.last_updated_at) or _parse_date_or_timestamp(other.created_at)
   140	            if other_upd_d is not None:
   141	                days_since_other = max(0, (ref_today - other_upd_d).days)
   142	                if days_since_other < neglect_window:
   143	                    relative_neglect = 1
   144	                    break
   145	
   146	    # 5. Untouched
   147	    untouched = 1 if (task.status == "todo" and task.status_changed_at == task.created_at) else 0
   148	
   149	    score = (
   150	        w_overdue * capped_overdue
   151	        + w_stale * capped_stale
   152	        + w_resched * resched_count
   153	        + w_neglect * relative_neglect
   154	        + w_untouched * untouched
   155	    )
   156	
   157	    # Synthesize evidence line deterministically (NO LLM, §14.3)
   158	    evidence_parts: list[str] = []
   159	    if resched_count > 0:
   160	        evidence_parts.append(f"Rescheduled {resched_count} time{'s' if resched_count > 1 else ''}")
   161	    if overdue_days > 0:
   162	        evidence_parts.append(f"overdue by {overdue_days} day{'s' if overdue_days > 1 else ''}")
   163	    if stale_days > 0:
   164	        evidence_parts.append(f"no updates for {stale_days} day{'s' if stale_days > 1 else ''}")
   165	    if relative_neglect == 1:
   166	        evidence_parts.append("inactive while other tasks are progressing")
   167	    if untouched == 1:
   168	        evidence_parts.append("not yet started")
   169	
   170	    if evidence_parts:
   171	        evidence_line = ", ".join(evidence_parts) + "."
   172	        evidence_line = evidence_line[0].upper() + evidence_line[1:]
   173	    else:
   174	        evidence_line = "No active stagnation signals."
   175	
   176	    signals = {
   177	        "overdue_days": overdue_days,
   178	        "capped_overdue": capped_overdue,
   179	        "stale_days": stale_days,
   180	        "capped_stale": capped_stale,
   181	        "reschedule_count": resched_count,
   182	        "relative_neglect": relative_neglect,
   183	        "untouched": untouched,
   184	        "score": score,
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    return score, evidence_line, signals
   188

   190	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191	# Question Draft & Profile Diff Helpers
   192	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   193	
   194	def generate_question_draft(task: Task, llm_client: Any | None = None) -> str:
   195	    """Generate an AI inquiry draft from task title and description (§14.4)."""
   196	    # Deterministic fallback template
   197	    desc_snippet = f": {task.description.strip()}" if task.description.strip() else ""
   198	    fallback = f"Seeking expertise and advice regarding {task.title}{desc_snippet}."
   199	
   200	    if llm_client is None:
   201	        return fallback
   202	
   203	    try:
   204	        model_name = os.environ.get("GEMINI_MODEL", "gemini-3.7-flash")
   205	        prompt = (
   206	            f"You are an AI assistant helping a colleague formulate a concise discovery inquiry.\n"
   207	            f"Task Title: {task.title}\n"
   208	            f"Task Description: {task.description}\n"
   209	            f"Write a single natural question (1-2 sentences in English) asking colleagues for advice or tacit knowledge to complete this task."
   210	        )
   211	        response = llm_client.models.generate_content(
   212	            model=model_name,
   213	            contents=prompt,
   214	        )
   215	        if hasattr(response, "text") and response.text:
   216	            cleaned = response.text.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
   217	            return cleaned if cleaned else fallback
   218	    except Exception:
   219	        pass
   220	
   221	    return fallback
   222	
   223	
   224	# Sentinel: LLM was configured but unreachable / returned garbage. Distinct from
   225	# None ("no diff") so the caller can leave the mail unconsumed and retry (R-2).
   226	EXTRACTION_FAILED = object()
   227	
   228	
   229	def extract_profile_diff(
   230	    mail: MailSeed,
   231	    current_profile: Profile | None = None,
   232	    llm_client: Any | None = None,
   233	) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
   234	    """Extract candidate profile item (item_key, body_draft) from unprocessed email (§14.5)."""
   235	    if not mail.body.strip() and not mail.subject.strip():
   236	        return None
   237	
   238	    if llm_client is not None:
   239	        try:
   240	            model_name = os.environ.get("GEMINI_MODEL", "gemini-3.7-flash")
   241	            existing_text = current_profile.get_full_text() if current_profile else ""
   242	            prompt = f"""You are analyzing a work email to see if it reveals tacit skills, current projects, or specialized background to add to the employee's professional profile.
   243	
   244	Current Profile:
   245	{existing_text}
   246	
   247	Email Subject: {mail.subject}
   248	Email Body: {mail.body}
   249	
   250	If this email contains meaningful professional experience or project context not yet in the profile, extract a suggested profile item in JSON format:
   251	{{
   252	  "item_key": "current_work" | "expertise" | "background",
   253	  "body_draft": "1-2 sentence description in English"
   254	}}
   255	If no meaningful knowledge is found, return null."""
   256	            response = llm_client.models.generate_content(
   257	                model=model_name,
   258	                contents=prompt,
   259	            )
   260	            resp_text = response.text.strip() if hasattr(response, "text") and response.text else ""
   261	            cleaned = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?\s*", "", resp_text)
   262	            cleaned = re.sub(r"\s*```$", "", cleaned).strip()
   263	            if cleaned == "null":
   264	                # Explicit "no diff": LLM was reachable and deliberately found
   265	                # nothing worth proposing. Do NOT fall through to the heuristic
   266	                # (design §14.5-1: null must be expressible; V-8/S-6).
   267	                return None
   268	            if not cleaned:
   269	                # Empty response is an API failure, not a deliberate null.
   270	                # Leave the mail unconsumed so the next sweep retries (R-2).
   271	                return EXTRACTION_FAILED
   272	            data = json.loads(cleaned)
   273	            if isinstance(data, dict) and "body_draft" in data:
   274	                key = data.get("item_key", "current_work")
   275	                body = data.get("body_draft", "").strip()
   276	                if body:
   277	                    return key, body
   278	            return None
   279	        except Exception:
   280	            # LLM call/parse failed. Do NOT copy mail text as a fallback (that
   281	            # re-opens S-6) and do NOT consume the mail — retry next sweep (R-2).
   282	            return EXTRACTION_FAILED
   283	
   284	    # Heuristic extraction fallback (reached only when llm_client is None,
   285	    # e.g. a local demo without GEMINI_API_KEY — never the default path, V-8/S-6).
   286	    subj = mail.subject.strip()
   287	    body = mail.body.strip()
   288	    if len(body) > 10:
   289	        return "current_work", f"{subj}: {body}"
   290	    return None

   299	
   300	    def __init__(

   297	class SecretaryService:
   298	    """Orchestrates secretary background sweep, morning digest, card CAS, and diff reviews."""
   299	
   300	    def __init__(
   301	        self,
   302	        store: Store,
   303	        kd_service: KnowledgeDiscoveryService,
   304	        matching_engine: MatchingEngine | None = None,
   305	        llm_client: Any | None = None,
   306	        t1: float = STAGNATION_T1,
   307	        t2: float = STAGNATION_T2,
   308	    ) -> None:
   309	        self.store = store
   310	        self.kd_service = kd_service
   311	        self.matching_engine = matching_engine or kd_service.matching_engine
   312	        self.llm_client = llm_client
   313	        self.t1 = t1
   314	        self.t2 = t2
   315	        assert self.t1 < self.t2, f"t1 ({self.t1}) must be < t2 ({self.t2})"
   316	
   317	    def run_sweep(self, demo_today: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
   318	        """Execute full secretary sweep across all tasks and mail seeds (§14.1, §14.2, §14.5).
   319	
   320	        State Machine Rules (§14.2):
   321	        1. Confirmed or Dismissed tasks: Do NOT recreate cards.
   322	        2. Task status 'done' or score < T1: Transition open card to 'resolved'.
   323	        3. T1 <= score < T2: Create or update 'notice' card.
   324	        4. score >= T2: Run preview search (public only, embedding_public). If candidates found,
   325	           create or promote card to 'request_draft'. If 0 candidates, keep/create 'notice'.
   326	        5. Idempotent: Never creates duplicate open cards for the same (owner, task_id).
   327	        """
   328	        ref_today = get_today(demo_today)
   329	        all_tasks = self.store.list_tasks()
   330	        registered_agents = self.store.list_agents(active_only=True)
   331	        profiles_list = self.store.list_profiles()
   332	        profiles_map = {p.employee_id: p for p in profiles_list}
   333	
   334	        # Group tasks by owner_employee_id
   335	        tasks_by_owner: dict[str, list[Task]] = {}
   336	        for t in all_tasks:
   337	            tasks_by_owner.setdefault(t.owner_employee_id, []).append(t)
   338	
   339	        tasks_evaluated = 0
   340	        cards_created = 0
   341	        cards_promoted = 0
   342	        cards_updated = 0
   343	        cards_resolved = 0
   344	        diff_cards_created = 0
   345	
   346	        for owner_id, owner_tasks in tasks_by_owner.items():
   347	            # Resolve sender entity for audit recording
   348	            owner_agent = self.store.get_agent_by_employee_id(owner_id)
   349	            sender_entity = owner_agent.agent_id if owner_agent else owner_id
   350	
   351	            for task in owner_tasks:
   352	                tasks_evaluated += 1
   353	
   354	                # Rule 1: Skip if already confirmed or dismissed
   355	                existing_cards = self.store.find_cards_for_task(owner_id, task.task_id)
   356	                has_terminal = any(c.status in ("confirmed", "dismissed") for c in existing_cards)
   357	                if has_terminal:
   358	                    continue
   359	
   360	                open_card = self.store.find_open_card_for_task(owner_id, task.task_id)
   361	
   362	                # Rule 2: If task is done -> resolve open card
   363	                if task.status == "done":
   364	                    if open_card is not None:
   365	                        open_card.status = "resolved"
   366	                        open_card.resolved_reason = "task_done"
   367	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   368	                        self.store.save_card(open_card)
   369	                        cards_resolved += 1
   370	                    continue
   371	
   372	                # Calculate score
   373	                score, evidence_line, _ = calculate_stagnation_score(
   374	                    task, owner_tasks, today=ref_today
   375	                )
   376	
   377	                # Rule 2b: If score < T1 -> resolve open card
   378	                if score < self.t1:
   379	                    if open_card is not None:
   380	                        open_card.status = "resolved"
   381	                        open_card.resolved_reason = "score_below_t1"
   382	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   383	                        self.store.save_card(open_card)
   384	                        cards_resolved += 1
   385	                    continue
   386	
   387	                # Rule 3: T1 <= score < T2 (Notice Tier)
   388	                if self.t1 <= score < self.t2:
   389	                    if open_card is None:
   390	                        card_id = f"card_stag_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}"
   391	                        new_card = Card(
   392	                            card_id=card_id,
   393	                            owner_employee_id=owner_id,
   394	                            type="stagnation",
   395	                            tier="notice",
   396	                            payload={
   397	                                "task_id": task.task_id,
   398	                                "task_title": task.title,
   399	                                "score": score,
   400	                                "evidence_line": evidence_line,
   401	                            },
   402	                            status="open",
   403	                        )
   404	                        self.store.save_card(new_card)
   405	                        cards_created += 1
   406	
   407	                        # Audit log (§14.6)
   408	                        self.kd_service.transmission.send(
   409	                            from_entity=sender_entity,
   410	                            to_entity="system",
   411	                            intent="stagnation_detected",
   412	                            payload_type="stagnation_detected",
   413	                            payload={"task_id": task.task_id, "tier": "notice", "score": score},
   414	                        )
   415	                    else:
   416	                        # Update existing open card without downgrading tier
   417	                        open_card.payload["score"] = score
   418	                        open_card.payload["evidence_line"] = evidence_line
   419	                        open_card.payload["task_title"] = task.title
   420	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   421	                        self.store.save_card(open_card)
   422	                        cards_updated += 1
   423	                    continue
   424	
   425	                # Rule 4: score >= T2 (Request Draft Tier or Notice fallback)
   426	                if score >= self.t2:
   427	                    if open_card is not None and open_card.tier == "request_draft":
   428	                        # No band change (already promoted, still >= T2): update
   429	                        # score/evidence_line only. Do NOT re-run preview search,
   430	                        # do NOT regenerate the question draft, and do NOT add a
   431	                        # new preview_search audit record (design §14.2 idempotency;
   432	                        # V-9/E-6: a re-sweep must not repeat side effects for an
   433	                        # already-promoted card).
   434	                        open_card.payload["score"] = score
   435	                        open_card.payload["evidence_line"] = evidence_line
   436	                        open_card.payload["task_title"] = task.title
   437	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   438	                        self.store.save_card(open_card)
   439	                        cards_updated += 1
   440	                        continue
   441	
   442	                    # 1. Draft inquiry question
   443	                    q_draft = generate_question_draft(task, llm_client=self.llm_client)
   444	
   445	                    # 2. Pure preview search on embedding_public
   446	                    preview_cands = self.matching_engine.preview_search(
   447	                        question=q_draft,
   448	                        registered_agents=registered_agents,
   449	                        profiles=profiles_map,
   450	                        exclude_employee_id=owner_id,
   451	                    )
   452	
   453	                    if len(preview_cands) == 0:
   454	                        tier = "notice"
   455	                        preview_dict: dict[str, Any] = {
   456	                            "candidates": [],
   457	                            "note": "No matching candidates found across public profiles.",
   458	                        }
   459	                    else:
   460	                        tier = "request_draft"
   461	                        preview_dict = {
   462	                            "candidates": [
   463	                                {
   464	                                    "employee_id": c.employee_id,
   465	                                    "name": c.name,
   466	                                    "reason_text": c.reason_text,
   467	                                    "score": c.score,
   468	                                    "cited_item_keys": c.cited_item_keys,
   469	                                }
   470	                                for c in preview_cands
   471	                            ]
   472	                        }
   473	
   474	                    if open_card is None:
   475	                        card_id = f"card_stag_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}"
   476	                        new_card = Card(
   477	                            card_id=card_id,
   478	                            owner_employee_id=owner_id,
   479	                            type="stagnation",
   480	                            tier=tier,
   481	                            payload={
   482	                                "task_id": task.task_id,
   483	                                "task_title": task.title,
   484	                                "score": score,
   485	                                "evidence_line": evidence_line,
   486	                                "question_draft": q_draft,
   487	                                "preview": preview_dict,
   488	                            },
   489	                            status="open",
   490	                        )
   491	                        self.store.save_card(new_card)
   492	                        cards_created += 1
   493	
   494	                        # Audit log: stagnation_detected
   495	                        self.kd_service.transmission.send(
   496	                            from_entity=sender_entity,
   497	                            to_entity="system",
   498	                            intent="stagnation_detected",
   499	                            payload_type="stagnation_detected",
   500	                            payload={"task_id": task.task_id, "tier": tier, "score": score},
   501	                        )
   502	
   503	                        # Audit log: preview_search if candidates found
   504	                        if preview_cands:
   505	                            self.kd_service.transmission.send(
   506	                                from_entity=sender_entity,
   507	                                to_entity="system",
   508	                                intent="preview_search",
   509	                                payload_type="preview_search",
   510	                                payload={
   511	                                    "task_id": task.task_id,
   512	                                    "candidates": [
   513	                                        {"employee_id": c.employee_id, "score": c.score}
   514	                                        for c in preview_cands
   515	                                    ],
   516	                                },
   517	                            )
   518	                    else:
   519	                        # Existing card: promote notice -> request_draft or update
   520	                        was_notice = (open_card.tier == "notice")
   521	                        open_card.tier = tier
   522	                        open_card.payload["score"] = score
   523	                        open_card.payload["evidence_line"] = evidence_line
   524	                        open_card.payload["task_title"] = task.title
   525	                        open_card.payload["question_draft"] = q_draft
   526	                        open_card.payload["preview"] = preview_dict
   527	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   528	                        self.store.save_card(open_card)
   529	
   530	                        if was_notice and tier == "request_draft":
   531	                            cards_promoted += 1
   532	                        else:
   533	                            cards_updated += 1
   534	
   535	                        # Record preview_search audit if preview executed
   536	                        if preview_cands:
   537	                            self.kd_service.transmission.send(
   538	                                from_entity=sender_entity,
   539	                                to_entity="system",
   540	                                intent="preview_search",
   541	                                payload_type="preview_search",
   542	                                payload={
   543	                                    "task_id": task.task_id,
   544	                                    "candidates": [
   545	                                        {"employee_id": c.employee_id, "score": c.score}
   546	                                        for c in preview_cands
   547	                                    ],
   548	                                },
   549	                            )
   550	
   551	        # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   552	        # Sweep Mail Seeds for Profile Diff Proposals (§14.5)
   553	        # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   554	        unprocessed_mails = self.store.list_mail_seeds(unprocessed_only=True)
   555	        for mail in unprocessed_mails:
   556	            owner_prof = self.store.get_profile(mail.owner_employee_id)
   557	            diff_res = extract_profile_diff(mail, owner_prof, llm_client=self.llm_client)
   558	
   559	            if diff_res is EXTRACTION_FAILED:
   560	                # LLM failure: leave processed=False so the next sweep retries (R-2)
   561	                continue
   562	
   563	            if diff_res is not None:
   564	                item_key, body_draft = diff_res
   565	                card_id = f"card_diff_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}"
   566	                diff_card = Card(
   567	                    card_id=card_id,
   568	                    owner_employee_id=mail.owner_employee_id,
   569	                    type="profile_diff",
   570	                    tier=None,
   571	                    payload={
   572	                        "item_key": item_key,
   573	                        "body_draft": body_draft,
   574	                        "source_mail_id": mail.mail_id,
   575	                        "subject": mail.subject,
   576	                    },
   577	                    status="open",
   578	                )
   579	                self.store.save_card(diff_card)
   580	                diff_cards_created += 1
   581	
   582	                owner_agent = self.store.get_agent_by_employee_id(mail.owner_employee_id)
   583	                sender_entity = owner_agent.agent_id if owner_agent else mail.owner_employee_id
   584	                self.kd_service.transmission.send(
   585	                    from_entity=sender_entity,
   586	                    to_entity="system",
   587	                    intent="profile_diff_proposed",
   588	                    payload_type="profile_diff_proposed",
   589	                    payload={"mail_id": mail.mail_id, "item_key": item_key},
   590	                )
   591	
   592	            # Mark processed
   593	            mail.processed = True
   594	            self.store.save_mail_seed(mail)
   595	
   596	        return {
   597	            "status": "ok",
   598	            "date": ref_today.isoformat(),
   599	            "tasks_evaluated": tasks_evaluated,
   600	            "cards_created": cards_created,
   601	            "cards_promoted": cards_promoted,
   602	            "cards_updated": cards_updated,
   603	            "cards_resolved": cards_resolved,
   604	            "diff_cards_created": diff_cards_created,
   605	        }
   606	
   607	    def get_morning_digest(
   608	        self,
   609	        employee_id: str,
   610	        demo_today: str | None = None,
   611	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   612	        """Assemble dynamic morning digest for employee (§14.2, §14.8).
   613	
   614	        Schedule rule order: Overdue -> Today -> Tomorrow -> Upcoming.
   615	        Followed by open stagnation and profile_diff cards.
   616	        """
   617	        ref_today = get_today(demo_today)
   618	        all_schedules = self.store.list_schedules(owner_employee_id=employee_id)
   619	
   620	        # Categorize and order schedule reminders

   607	    def get_morning_digest(
   608	        self,
   609	        employee_id: str,
   610	        demo_today: str | None = None,
   611	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   612	        """Assemble dynamic morning digest for employee (§14.2, §14.8).
   613	
   614	        Schedule rule order: Overdue -> Today -> Tomorrow -> Upcoming.
   615	        Followed by open stagnation and profile_diff cards.
   616	        """
   617	        ref_today = get_today(demo_today)
   618	        all_schedules = self.store.list_schedules(owner_employee_id=employee_id)
   619	
   620	        # Categorize and order schedule reminders
   621	        reminders: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   622	        for s in all_schedules:
   623	            due_d = _parse_date_or_timestamp(s.due_date)
   624	            if due_d is None:
   625	                category = "upcoming"
   626	                priority = 4
   627	            elif due_d < ref_today:
   628	                category = "overdue"
   629	                priority = 1
   630	            elif due_d == ref_today:
   631	                category = "today"
   632	                priority = 2
   633	            elif (due_d - ref_today).days == 1:
   634	                category = "tomorrow"
   635	                priority = 3
   636	            else:
   637	                category = "upcoming"
   638	                priority = 4
   639	
   640	            reminders.append({
   641	                "item_id": s.item_id,
   642	                "kind": s.kind,
   643	                "title": s.title,
   644	                "due_date": s.due_date,
   645	                "due_category": category,
   646	                "_priority": priority,
   647	            })
   648	
   649	        # Sort: priority ascending, then due_date ascending
   650	        reminders.sort(key=lambda r: (r["_priority"], r["due_date"]))
   651	        for r in reminders:
   652	            r.pop("_priority", None)
   653	
   654	        # Retrieve open cards
   655	        open_cards = self.store.list_cards(owner_employee_id=employee_id, status="open")
   656	        stagnation_cards = [
   657	            c.to_dict() for c in open_cards if c.type == "stagnation"
   658	        ]
   659	        profile_diff_cards = [
   660	            c.to_dict() for c in open_cards if c.type == "profile_diff"
   661	        ]
   662	
   663	        return {
   664	            "employee_id": employee_id,
   665	            "date": ref_today.isoformat(),
   666	            "reminders": reminders,
   667	            "stagnation_cards": stagnation_cards,
   668	            "profile_diff_cards": profile_diff_cards,
   669	        }
   670	
   671	    def confirm_stagnation_card(
   672	        self,
   673	        card_id: str,
   674	        edited_question: str,
   675	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   676	        """Confirm stagnation card and dispatch query via standard query path (§14.4).
   677	
   678	        Atomic CAS (X-3, V-6/S-8):
   679	        - Store.try_confirm_card() atomically transitions open->confirmed; only the
   680	          caller that wins the race proceeds to submit the query.
   681	        - A losing concurrent call (card already 'confirmed') returns the existing
   682	          linked_query_audit_id without resubmitting.
   683	        - If not 'open' and not 'confirmed', raises an error.
   684	        - On query execution failure, rolls back card status to 'open'.
   685	        """
   686	        card, won = self.store.try_confirm_card(card_id)
   687	        if card is None:
   688	            raise ValueError(f"Secretary card '{card_id}' not found")
   689	
   690	        if not won:
   691	            if card.status == "confirmed":
   692	                return {
   693	                    "status": "already_confirmed",
   694	                    "card_id": card.card_id,
   695	                    "query_audit_id": card.linked_query_audit_id,
   696	                }
   697	            raise ValueError(f"Card '{card_id}' cannot be confirmed in status '{card.status}'")
   698	
   699	        try:
   700	            # Pass edited question to existing query pipeline
   701	            result = self.kd_service.submit_query(
   702	                requester_id=card.owner_employee_id,
   703	                question_text=edited_question,
   704	            )
   705	            query_audit_id = result.query_message.audit_id
   706	            card.linked_query_audit_id = query_audit_id
   707	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   708	            self.store.save_card(card)
   709	
   710	            return {
   711	                "status": "confirmed",
   712	                "card_id": card.card_id,
   713	                "query_audit_id": query_audit_id,
   714	                "dispatched_count": len(result.dispatched_asks),
   715	            }
   716	        except Exception as exc:
   717	            # Rollback on submission failure
   718	            card.status = "open"
   719	            card.linked_query_audit_id = None
   720	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   721	            self.store.save_card(card)
   722	            raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to submit discovery query: {exc}") from exc
   723	
   724	    def review_profile_diff(
   725	        self,
   726	        card_id: str,
   727	        action: str,  # 'apply' | 'edit_apply' | 'private_apply' | 'dismiss'
   728	        edited_body: str | None = None,
   729	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   730	        """Process employee review for a profile diff proposal card (§14.5).
   731	
   732	        Actions:
   733	        - 'apply': Add a NEW item with visibility='public', regenerate embeddings, card -> 'applied'.
   734	        - 'edit_apply': Add a NEW item with the edited body (public), regenerate embeddings, card -> 'applied'.
   735	        - 'private_apply': Add a NEW item with visibility='private', regenerate embeddings, card -> 'applied'.
   736	        - 'dismiss': Card -> 'dismissed' (no profile change).
   737	
   738	        Reflection is always additive (design §14.5-3, V-7/S-7): existing profile
   739	        items are never overwritten and their visibility is never flipped. If the
   740	        proposed item_key collides with an existing item, a unique suffixed key is
   741	        used instead. The item_key itself always comes from the card's own payload
   742	        (never from the caller) so a request cannot retarget which item gets edited.
   743	        """
   744	        card = self.store.get_card(card_id)
   745	        if card is None or card.type != "profile_diff":
   746	            raise ValueError(f"Profile diff card '{card_id}' not found")
   747	        if card.status != "open":
   748	            raise ValueError(f"Card '{card_id}' is already {card.status}")
   749	
   750	        if action == "dismiss":
   751	            card.status = "dismissed"
   752	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   753	            self.store.save_card(card)
   754	            return {"status": "dismissed", "card_id": card_id}
   755	
   756	        if action not in ("apply", "edit_apply", "private_apply"):
   757	            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported review action '{action}'")
   758	
   759	        key = card.payload.get("item_key", "current_work")
   760	        body = (
   761	            edited_body.strip()
   762	            if (action == "edit_apply" and edited_body and edited_body.strip())
   763	            else str(card.payload.get("body_draft", "")).strip()
   764	        )
   765	        visibility = "private" if action == "private_apply" else "public"
   766	
   767	        # Reflection assumes the profile already exists (V-10): the secretary
   768	        # never fabricates a profile for a mail owner who isn't a registered
   769	        # employee.
   770	        prof = self.store.get_profile(card.owner_employee_id)
   771	        if prof is None:
   772	            raise LookupError(
   773	                f"Profile for employee '{card.owner_employee_id}' not found; cannot apply diff"
   774	            )
   775	
   776	        # Additive only: never overwrite an existing key/visibility. On
   777	        # collision, append a uniquely suffixed key instead (design §14.5-3).
   778	        if prof.get_item(key) is not None:
   779	            suffix = 1
   780	            candidate_key = f"{key}_mail_{suffix}"
   781	            while prof.get_item(candidate_key) is not None:
   782	                suffix += 1
   783	                candidate_key = f"{key}_mail_{suffix}"
   784	            key = candidate_key
   785	
   786	        new_item = ProfileItem(
   787	            key=key,
   788	            body=body,
   789	            source="mail_seed",
   790	            visibility=visibility,
   791	            reviewed=True,
   792	        )
   793	        prof.items.append(new_item)
   794	
   795	        # Regenerate BOTH full embedding and public embedding (§14.5, §9.3)
   796	        self.matching_engine.compute_profile_embedding(prof)
   797	        self.store.save_profile(prof)
   798	
   799	        # Update card status
   800	        card.status = "applied"
   801	        card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   802	        self.store.save_card(card)
   803	
   804	        return {
   805	            "status": "applied",
   806	            "card_id": card_id,
   807	            "item_key": key,
   808	            "visibility": visibility,
   809	            "reviewed": True,
   810	        }
   811	
   812	    def dismiss_card(self, card_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   813	        """Dismiss an open stagnation card (§14.2, E-8).
   814	
   815	        Restricted to type='stagnation' and status='open': profile_diff cards
   816	        have their own dedicated 'see later' path via review_profile_diff(action='dismiss'),
   817	        and confirmed/applied/resolved cards must not be reachable through this
   818	        generic endpoint.
   819	        """
   820	        card = self.store.get_card(card_id)
   821	        if card is None or card.type != "stagnation":
   822	            raise ValueError(f"Stagnation card '{card_id}' not found")
   823	        if card.status != "open":
   824	            raise ValueError(f"Card '{card_id}' cannot be dismissed in status '{card.status}'")
   825	        card.status = "dismissed"
   826	        card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   827	        self.store.save_card(card)
   828	        return {"status": "dismissed", "card_id": card_id}
     1	"""HTTP client for the Cloud Run knowledge-discovery secretary API.
     2	
     3	This package (src/secretary_agent) is deliberately independent of
     4	src/knowledge_discovery: it only talks to the already-deployed Cloud Run
     5	service over its public HTTP API (X-API-Key auth), exactly like any other
     6	GEAP-registered client would (design.md §14.7 "パッケージング").
     7	"""
     8	
     9	from __future__ import annotations
    10	
    11	import os
    12	from typing import Any
    13	
    14	import requests
    15	
    16	
    17	class SecretaryApiError(RuntimeError):
    18	    """Raised when the Cloud Run secretary API returns a non-2xx response or
    19	    is unreachable.
    20	
    21	    Callers (app.py's run_daily_sweep) must let this propagate rather than
    22	    swallow it: design §14.7 入口1 requires Cloud Run failures to make the
    23	    Agent Engine `:query` response itself non-2xx, so Cloud Scheduler can
    24	    detect and retry the failed sweep (C-31: failures are not silenced).
    25	    """
    26	
    27	
    28	class SecretaryApiClient:
    29	    """Thin client for POST /api/secretary/sweep and GET /api/secretary/digest."""
    30	
    31	    def __init__(
    32	        self,
    33	        base_url: str | None = None,
    34	        api_key: str | None = None,
    35	        session: requests.Session | None = None,
    36	        timeout: float | None = None,
    37	    ) -> None:
    38	        self._base_url = (base_url or os.environ["KD_API_BASE_URL"]).rstrip("/")
    39	        self._api_key = api_key or os.environ["KD_API_KEY"]
    40	        # Injectable so tests can substitute a fake Session instead of making
    41	        # real network calls.
    42	        self._session = session or requests.Session()
    43	        # B-1 (round-11): the first sweep after a reseed took ~31s in production
    44	        # (embedding_public + LLM question draft), so 30s produced a false
    45	        # failure. Default 120s stays inside the Scheduler attempt deadline (180s).
    46	        self._timeout = timeout if timeout is not None else float(os.environ.get("KD_API_TIMEOUT", "120"))
    47	
    48	    def _headers(self) -> dict[str, str]:
    49	        return {"X-API-Key": self._api_key}
    50	
    51	    def run_sweep(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
    52	        """POST /api/secretary/sweep (design §14.1, §14.7 入口1).
    53	
    54	        Raises SecretaryApiError on a non-2xx response or connection
    55	        failure.
    56	        """
    57	        try:
    58	            resp = self._session.post(
    59	                f"{self._base_url}/api/secretary/sweep",
    60	                headers=self._headers(),
    61	                timeout=self._timeout,
    62	            )
    63	        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as exc:
    64	            raise SecretaryApiError(f"sweep request failed: {exc}") from exc
    65	        if not (200 <= resp.status_code < 300):
    66	            raise SecretaryApiError(
    67	                f"sweep returned HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}"
    68	            )
    69	        return resp.json()
    70	
    71	    def fetch_digest(self, employee_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    72	        """GET /api/secretary/digest?employee_id=<employee_id> (design §14.2, §14.8).
    73	
    74	        Raises SecretaryApiError on a non-2xx response or connection
    75	        failure.
    76	        """
    77	        try:
    78	            resp = self._session.get(
    79	                f"{self._base_url}/api/secretary/digest",
    80	                params={"employee_id": employee_id},
    81	                headers=self._headers(),
    82	                timeout=self._timeout,
    83	            )
    84	        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as exc:
    85	            raise SecretaryApiError(f"digest request failed: {exc}") from exc
    86	        if not (200 <= resp.status_code < 300):
    87	            raise SecretaryApiError(
    88	                f"digest returned HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}"
    89	            )
    90	        return resp.json()

     1	"""Gemini API adapters for vector embedding and candidate-isolated connection inference.
     2	
     3	Follows design.md §2:
     4	- GeminiEmbedder: Embedder implementation using Gemini embedding models.
     5	- GeminiConnectionInferencer: Stage 2 candidate-isolated connection inference using Gemini 3.7 Flash.
     6	- Strict requirement: Candidate receives ONLY the question and their own profile.
     7	- Prompts explicitly allow connection: null ("意味のある接点が見つからなければ connection: null を返してよい（無理に理由をひねり出さない）").
     8	- Structured JSON output with robust fallback on parse failure.
     9	- API keys read from GEMINI_API_KEY environment variable (no hardcoded keys).
    10	"""
    11	
    12	from __future__ import annotations
    13

    15	import math
    16	import os
    17	import re

    29	    from google import genai

    35	
    36	def _build_genai_client(api_key: str) -> Any | None:
    37	    """Build a genai.Client for either Vertex AI mode or API-key mode.
    38	
    39	    Vertex AI mode (GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI=true + ADC credentials) needs no
    40	    API key and bills the Cloud project — used because AI Studio keys default
    41	    to a prepaid plan that cannot be switched to postpaid billing.
    42	    """
    43	    if genai is None:
    44	        return None
    45	    if os.environ.get("GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI", "").lower() in ("1", "true"):
    46	        return genai.Client()
    47	    if api_key:
    48	        return genai.Client(api_key=api_key)
    49	    return None

    52	class GeminiEmbedder(Embedder):
    53	    """Vector embedder powered by Gemini embedding models."""
    54	
    55	    def __init__(
    56	        self,
    57	        api_key: str | None = None,
    58	        model: str | None = None,
    59	        client: Any | None = None,
    60	    ) -> None:
    61	        """Initialize GeminiEmbedder.
    62	
    63	        Args:
    64	            api_key: Gemini API key. Defaults to GEMINI_API_KEY env var.
    65	            model: Embedding model ID. Defaults to GEMINI_EMBEDDING_MODEL or 'text-embedding-004'.
    66	            client: Optional pre-configured genai.Client instance (useful for testing/mocking).
    67	        """
    68	        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY", "")
    69	        # text-embedding-004 was retired; gemini-embedding-2 is the current GA model
    70	        self.model = model or os.environ.get("GEMINI_EMBEDDING_MODEL", "gemini-embedding-2")
    71	        if client is not None:
    72	            self.client = client
    73	        else:
    74	            self.client = _build_genai_client(self.api_key)
    75	
    76	    def embed(self, text: str) -> list[float]:
    77	        """Generate normalized embedding vector for text using Gemini API."""
    78	        if not text or not text.strip():
    79	            return []
    80	
    81	        if self.client is None:
    82	            raise RuntimeError(
    83	                "Gemini client is not initialized. Ensure GEMINI_API_KEY is set or google-genai is installed."
    84	            )
    85	
    86	        response = None
    87	        last_exc: Exception | None = None
    88	        for attempt in range(4):
    89	            try:
    90	                # google-genai SDK call
    91	                response = self.client.models.embed_content(
    92	                    model=self.model,
    93	                    contents=text,
    94	                )
    95	                break
    96	            except Exception as exc:
    97	                # Free-tier rate limit (100 req/min): wait out the window and retry
    98	                if "RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED" in str(exc) or "429" in str(exc):
    99	                    last_exc = exc
   100	                    time.sleep(62)
   101	                    continue
   102	                raise RuntimeError(f"Gemini embedding API call failed: {exc}") from exc
   103	        if response is None:
   104	            raise RuntimeError(f"Gemini embedding API call failed after retries: {last_exc}") from last_exc
   105	
   106	        try:
   107	            # Extract vector values from SDK response
   108	            values: list[float] = []
   109	            if hasattr(response, "embedding") and hasattr(response.embedding, "values"):
   110	                values = [float(v) for v in response.embedding.values]
   111	            elif hasattr(response, "embeddings") and len(response.embeddings) > 0:
   112	                values = [float(v) for v in response.embeddings[0].values]
   113	            elif isinstance(response, dict):
   114	                if "embedding" in response and "values" in response["embedding"]:
   115	                    values = [float(v) for v in response["embedding"]["values"]]
   116	                elif "embeddings" in response and len(response["embeddings"]) > 0:
   117	                    values = [float(v) for v in response["embeddings"][0]["values"]]
   118	
   119	            # L2 normalize
   120	            norm = math.sqrt(sum(v * v for v in values))
   121	            if norm > 0.0:
   122	                return [v / norm for v in values]
   123	            return values
   124	        except Exception as exc:
   125	            raise RuntimeError(f"Gemini embedding API call failed: {exc}") from exc
   126	
   127	    def similarity(self, vec_a: list[float], vec_b: list[float]) -> float:
   128	        """Compute cosine similarity between two normalized vectors, clamped to [0.0, 1.0]."""
   129	        if not vec_a or not vec_b or len(vec_a) != len(vec_b):
   130	            return 0.0
   131	        dot = sum(a * b for a, b in zip(vec_a, vec_b))
   132	        return max(0.0, min(1.0, float(dot)))
   133	
   134	
   135	class GeminiConnectionInferencer(ConnectionInferencer):
   136	    """Stage 2 candidate-isolated connection inferencer using Gemini 3.7 Flash (C-17)."""
   137	
   138	    SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are an expert AI knowledge broker analyzing tacit and explicit connections between a colleague's inquiry and a candidate's background.
   139	
   140	Critical Rules:
   141	1. Candidate Isolation: You receive ONLY the inquiry question and this single candidate's profile items.
   142	2. Connection Reasoning: Identify direct and indirect/tacit connections (e.g. past career experience, related problem spaces, specialized domain context, client nuances).
   143	3. Grounding & Citation: You MUST list the exact `cited_item_keys` (from the candidate's profile items) that support your reasoning.
   144	4. Permission to Disclaim: 意味のある接点が見つからなければ connection: null を返してよい（無理に理由をひねり出さない）. If no meaningful, actionable connection exists, return `connection: null` and explain why in `no_connection_reason`.
   145	5. Score: When connection is found, provide a confidence score between 0.0 and 1.0 (typical strong match is >= 0.70).
   146	
   147	Output JSON Schema:
   148	{
   149	  "connection": {
   150	    "reason_text": "string explaining the verbalized connection in concise English",
   151	    "score": 0.85
   152	  } | null,
   153	  "no_connection_reason": "string explaining why there is no meaningful connection (if connection is null)" | null,
   154	  "cited_item_keys": ["key1", "key2"]
   155	}
   156	"""
   157	
   158	    def __init__(
   159	        self,
   160	        api_key: str | None = None,
   161	        model: str | None = None,
   162	        client: Any | None = None,
   163	    ) -> None:
   164	        """Initialize GeminiConnectionInferencer.
   165	
   166	        Args:
   167	            api_key: Gemini API key. Defaults to GEMINI_API_KEY env var.
   168	            model: Gemini model ID. Defaults to GEMINI_MODEL or 'gemini-3.7-flash'.
   169	            client: Optional pre-configured genai.Client instance for testing.
   170	        """
   171	        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY", "")
   172	        self.model = model or os.environ.get("GEMINI_MODEL", "gemini-3.7-flash")
   173	        if client is not None:
   174	            self.client = client
   175	        else:
   176	            self.client = _build_genai_client(self.api_key)
   177	
   178	    def _format_profile_context(self, profile: Profile) -> str:
   179	        """Format candidate profile items into text for isolated inference."""
   180	        lines = [
   181	            f"Candidate: {profile.name} ({profile.role})",
   182	            "Profile Items:",
   183	        ]
   184	        for item in profile.items:
   185	            lines.append(f"- [{item.key}] (visibility={item.visibility}): {item.body}")
   186	        return "\n".join(lines)
   187	
   188	    def infer_connection(self, question: str, profile: Profile) -> ConnectionInferenceResult:
   189	        """Perform isolated Stage 2 connection inference for a single candidate."""
   190	        if self.client is None:
   191	            raise RuntimeError(
   192	                "Gemini client is not initialized. Ensure GEMINI_API_KEY is set or google-genai is installed."
   193	            )
   194	
   195	        candidate_context = self._format_profile_context(profile)
   196	        user_prompt = f"""Inquiry Question:
   197	\"{question}\"
   198	
   199	{candidate_context}
   200	
   201	Analyze if this candidate has relevant knowledge or background for the inquiry.
   202	Output strictly valid JSON matching the required schema.
   203	意味のある接点が見つからなければ connection: null を返してよい（無理に理由をひねり出さない）。"""
   204	
   205	        all_item_keys = [item.key for item in profile.items]
   206	
   207	        try:
   208	            # Set up config with structured JSON output if types module available
   209	            config_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {}
   210	            if types is not None and hasattr(types, "GenerateContentConfig"):
   211	                config_kwargs["config"] = types.GenerateContentConfig(
   212	                    system_instruction=self.SYSTEM_PROMPT,
   213	                    response_mime_type="application/json",
   214	                    temperature=0.2,
   215	                )
   216	
   217	            response = self.client.models.generate_content(
   218	                model=self.model,
   219	                contents=user_prompt,
   220	                **config_kwargs,
   221	            )
   222	
   223	            response_text = ""
   224	            if hasattr(response, "text") and response.text:
   225	                response_text = response.text
   226	            elif isinstance(response, dict) and "text" in response:
   227	                response_text = response["text"]
   228	            elif isinstance(response, str):
   229	                response_text = response
   230	
   231	            # Clean code fences if present
   232	            cleaned_text = response_text.strip()
   233	            if cleaned_text.startswith("```"):
   234	                cleaned_text = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?\s*", "", cleaned_text)
   235	                cleaned_text = re.sub(r"\s*```$", "", cleaned_text)
   236	
   237	            data = json.loads(cleaned_text)
   238	            return self._parse_json_result(data, all_item_keys)
   239	
   240	        except Exception as exc:
   241	            # Fallback to no_connection on API or parsing failure (fail-closed)
   242	            first_key = []  # V-1: never fabricate a citation key
   243	            return ConnectionInferenceResult(
   244	                connection=None,
   245	                no_connection_reason=f"Inference evaluation unavailable: {exc}",
   246	                cited_item_keys=first_key,
   247	            )
   248	
   249	    def _parse_json_result(
   250	        self, data: dict[str, Any], all_item_keys: list[str]
   251	    ) -> ConnectionInferenceResult:
   252	        """Parse dictionary into ConnectionInferenceResult with strict validation."""
   253	        cited_keys = data.get("cited_item_keys")
   254	        if not isinstance(cited_keys, list):
   255	            # Do NOT default to a profile key (V-1: a fabricated public key would
   256	            # bypass the private mask). Missing citations stay empty; the
   257	            # transmission layer's reason-text scan is the safety net.
   258	            cited_keys = []
   259	
   260	        connection_data = data.get("connection")
   261	        if isinstance(connection_data, dict):
   262	            reason_text = str(connection_data.get("reason_text", "")).strip()
   263	            try:
   264	                score = float(connection_data.get("score", 0.8))
   265	            except (ValueError, TypeError):
   266	                score = 0.8
   267	
   268	            if reason_text:
   269	                return ConnectionInferenceResult(
   270	                    connection=ConnectionDetails(reason_text=reason_text, score=score),
   271	                    no_connection_reason=None,
   272	                    cited_item_keys=[str(k) for k in cited_keys],
   273	                )
   274	
   275	        # Connection is null or invalid
   276	        no_conn_reason = data.get("no_connection_reason") or "No meaningful connection found"
   277	        return ConnectionInferenceResult(
   278	            connection=None,
   279	            no_connection_reason=str(no_conn_reason),
   280	            cited_item_keys=[str(k) for k in cited_keys],
     1	"""Firestore implementation of the Store interface for knowledge discovery.
     2	
     3	Follows design.md §3:
     4	- agents collection: agents/{agent_id}
     5	- profiles collection: profiles/{employee_id}
     6	- messages collection: messages/{audit_id}
     7	- In-memory vector similarity computation over fetched profiles (avoiding composite index validation uncertainties).
     8	"""

    14	from knowledge_discovery.models import (
    15	    Agent,

    26	try:
    27	    from google.cloud import firestore
    28	except ImportError:
    29	    firestore = None  # type: ignore[assignment]
