    31	
    32	class FirestoreStore(Store):
    33	    """Google Cloud Firestore implementation of Store interface."""
    34	
    35	    def __init__(
    36	        self,
    37	        client: Any | None = None,
    38	        project: str | None = None,
    39	        database: str | None = None,
    40	    ) -> None:
    41	        """Initialize FirestoreStore with either an injected client or new Firestore Client."""
    42	        if client is not None:
    43	            self.db = client
    44	        elif firestore is not None:
    45	            self.db = firestore.Client(project=project, database=database)
    46	        else:
    47	            raise RuntimeError(
    48	                "google-cloud-firestore is not installed and no custom client was provided."
    49	            )
    50	
    51	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52	    # Agent operations (agents collection / registry)
    53	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54	
    55	    def save_agent(self, agent: Agent) -> None:
    56	        """Save or update an agent record in the agents collection."""
    57	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("agents").document(agent.agent_id)
    58	        doc_ref.set(agent.to_dict())
    59	
    60	    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    61	        """Retrieve an agent by agent_id."""
    62	        doc = self.db.collection("agents").document(agent_id).get()
    63	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
    64	            return None
    65	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
    66	        if not data:
    67	            return None
    68	        return Agent.from_dict(data)
    69	
    70	    def get_agent_by_employee_id(self, employee_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    71	        """Retrieve an agent by employee_id."""
    72	        # Query agents collection for matching employee_id
    73	        col = self.db.collection("agents")
    74	        docs = list(col.where("employee_id", "==", employee_id).limit(1).stream())
    75	        if not docs:
    76	            return None
    77	        doc = docs[0]
    78	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
    79	        if not data:
    80	            return None
    81	        return Agent.from_dict(data)
    82	
    83	    def list_agents(self, active_only: bool = False) -> list[Agent]:
    84	        """List registered agents, optionally filtering for active only."""
    85	        col = self.db.collection("agents")
    86	        if active_only:
    87	            docs = col.where("active", "==", True).stream()
    88	        else:
    89	            docs = col.stream()
    90	
    91	        agents: list[Agent] = []
    92	        for doc in docs:
    93	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
    94	            if data:
    95	                agents.append(Agent.from_dict(data))
    96	        return agents
    97	
    98	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    99	    # Profile operations (profiles collection)
   100	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101	
   102	    def save_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> None:
   103	        """Save or update an employee knowledge profile."""
   104	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("profiles").document(profile.employee_id)
   105	        doc_ref.set(profile.to_dict())
   106	
   107	    def get_profile(self, employee_id: str) -> Profile | None:
   108	        """Retrieve a profile by employee_id."""
   109	        doc = self.db.collection("profiles").document(employee_id).get()
   110	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
   111	            return None
   112	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   113	        if not data:
   114	            return None
   115	        return Profile.from_dict(data)
   116	
   117	    def list_profiles(self) -> list[Profile]:
   118	        """List all employee profiles."""
   119	        col = self.db.collection("profiles")
   120	        docs = col.stream()
   121	        profiles: list[Profile] = []
   122	        for doc in docs:
   123	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   124	            if data:
   125	                profiles.append(Profile.from_dict(data))
   126	        return profiles
   127	
   128	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129	    # Message operations (messages collection / audit log)
   130	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   131	
   132	    def save_message(self, message: Message) -> None:
   133	        """Save or update a message record."""
   134	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("messages").document(message.audit_id)
   135	        doc_ref.set(message.to_dict())
   136	
   137	    def get_message(self, audit_id: str) -> Message | None:
   138	        """Retrieve a message by audit_id."""
   139	        doc = self.db.collection("messages").document(audit_id).get()
   140	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
   141	            return None
   142	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   143	        if not data:
   144	            return None
   145	        return Message.from_dict(data)
   146	
   147	    def list_messages(self, limit: int | None = None) -> list[Message]:
   148	        """List all messages in chronological order."""
   149	        col = self.db.collection("messages")
   150	        # In Firestore, retrieve and sort in-memory or by timestamp
   151	        docs = col.stream()
   152	        messages: list[Message] = []
   153	        for doc in docs:
   154	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   155	            if data:
   156	                messages.append(Message.from_dict(data))
   157	
   158	        # Sort chronologically by timestamp
   159	        messages.sort(key=lambda m: m.timestamp)
   160	
   161	        if limit is not None and limit > 0:
   162	            return messages[-limit:]
   163	        return messages
   164	
   165	    def get_messages_for_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> list[Message]:
   166	        """Retrieve messages involving the given entity (sender, recipient, or participant)."""
   167	        all_msgs = self.list_messages()
   168	        results: list[Message] = []
   169	        for msg in all_msgs:
   170	            participants = (
   171	                msg.payload.get("participants", []) if isinstance(msg.payload, dict) else []
   172	            )
   173	            if (
   174	                msg.from_entity == entity_id
   175	                or msg.to_entity == entity_id
   176	                or entity_id in participants
   177	            ):
   178	                results.append(msg)
   179	        return results
   180	
   181	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   182	    # Task operations (tasks collection)
   183	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   184	
   185	    def save_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
   186	        """Save or update a task record."""
   187	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("tasks").document(task.task_id)
   188	        doc_ref.set(task.to_dict())
   189	
   190	    def list_tasks(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Task]:
   191	        """List tasks, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""
   192	        col = self.db.collection("tasks")
   193	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   194	            docs = col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id).stream()
   195	        else:
   196	            docs = col.stream()
   197	        tasks: list[Task] = []
   198	        for doc in docs:
   199	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   200	            if data:
   201	                tasks.append(Task.from_dict(data))
   202	        return tasks
   203	
   204	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   205	    # Schedule operations (schedules collection)
   206	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   207	
   208	    def save_schedule(self, schedule: Schedule) -> None:
   209	        """Save or update a schedule reminder."""
   210	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("schedules").document(schedule.item_id)
   211	        doc_ref.set(schedule.to_dict())
   212	
   213	    def list_schedules(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Schedule]:
   214	        """List schedules, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""
   215	        col = self.db.collection("schedules")
   216	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   217	            docs = col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id).stream()
   218	        else:
   219	            docs = col.stream()
   220	        schedules: list[Schedule] = []
   221	        for doc in docs:
   222	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   223	            if data:
   224	                schedules.append(Schedule.from_dict(data))
   225	        return schedules
   226	
   227	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   228	    # MailSeed operations (mail_seeds collection)
   229	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   230	
   231	    def save_mail_seed(self, mail: MailSeed) -> None:
   232	        """Save or update an email seed record."""
   233	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("mail_seeds").document(mail.mail_id)
   234	        doc_ref.set(mail.to_dict())
   235	
   236	    def get_mail_seed(self, mail_id: str) -> MailSeed | None:
   237	        """Retrieve an email seed by mail_id."""
   238	        doc = self.db.collection("mail_seeds").document(mail_id).get()
   239	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
   240	            return None
   241	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   242	        if not data:
   243	            return None
   244	        return MailSeed.from_dict(data)
   245	
   246	    def list_mail_seeds(
   247	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, unprocessed_only: bool = False
   248	    ) -> list[MailSeed]:
   249	        """List mail seeds, optionally filtered by owner and/or unprocessed status."""
   250	        col = self.db.collection("mail_seeds")
   251	        query = col
   252	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   253	            query = query.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
   254	        if unprocessed_only:
   255	            query = query.where("processed", "==", False)
   256	        docs = query.stream()
   257	        mails: list[MailSeed] = []
   258	        for doc in docs:
   259	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   260	            if data:
   261	                mails.append(MailSeed.from_dict(data))
   262	        return mails
   263	
   264	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   265	    # Card operations (cards collection)
   266	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   267	
   268	    def save_card(self, card: Card) -> None:
   269	        """Save or update a secretary card."""
   270	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("cards").document(card.card_id)
   271	        doc_ref.set(card.to_dict())
   272	
   273	    def get_card(self, card_id: str) -> Card | None:
   274	        """Retrieve a card by card_id."""
   275	        doc = self.db.collection("cards").document(card_id).get()
   276	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
   277	            return None
   278	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   279	        if not data:
   280	            return None
   281	        return Card.from_dict(data)
   282	
   283	    def list_cards(
   284	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, status: str | None = None
   285	    ) -> list[Card]:
   286	        """List cards, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id and/or status."""
   287	        col = self.db.collection("cards")
   288	        query = col
   289	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   290	            query = query.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
   291	        if status is not None:
   292	            query = query.where("status", "==", status)
   293	        docs = query.stream()
   294	        cards: list[Card] = []
   295	        for doc in docs:
   296	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   297	            if data:
   298	                cards.append(Card.from_dict(data))
   299	        return cards
   300	
   301	    def find_open_card_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> Card | None:
   302	        """Find an open stagnation card for the given task and owner."""
   303	        col = self.db.collection("cards")
   304	        docs = (
   305	            col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
   306	            .where("type", "==", "stagnation")
   307	            .where("status", "==", "open")
   308	            .where("payload.task_id", "==", task_id)
   309	            .limit(1)
   310	            .stream()
   311	        )
   312	        for doc in docs:
   313	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   314	            if data:
   315	                return Card.from_dict(data)
   316	        return None
   317	
   318	    def find_cards_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> list[Card]:
   319	        """Find all stagnation cards for the given task and owner."""
   320	        col = self.db.collection("cards")
   321	        docs = (
   322	            col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
   323	            .where("type", "==", "stagnation")
   324	            .where("payload.task_id", "==", task_id)
   325	            .stream()
   326	        )
   327	        cards: list[Card] = []
   328	        for doc in docs:
   329	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   330	            if data:
   331	                cards.append(Card.from_dict(data))
   332	        return cards
   333	
   334	    def try_confirm_card(self, card_id: str) -> tuple[Card | None, bool]:
   335	        """Atomically transition a card's status from 'open' to 'confirmed' (CAS, §14.4)."""
   336	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("cards").document(card_id)
   337	        transaction = self.db.transaction()
   338	
   339	        @firestore.transactional
   340	        def _txn(txn: Any) -> tuple[Card | None, bool]:
   341	            snapshot = doc_ref.get(transaction=txn)
   342	            if hasattr(snapshot, "exists") and not snapshot.exists:
   343	                return None, False
   344	            data = snapshot.to_dict() if hasattr(snapshot, "to_dict") else None
   345	            if not data:
   346	                return None, False
   347	            card = Card.from_dict(data)
   348	            if card.status != "open":
   349	                return card, False
   350	            card.status = "confirmed"
   351	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   352	            txn.set(doc_ref, card.to_dict())
   353	            return card, True
   354	
   355	        return _txn(transaction)
   356	
   357	    def clear(self) -> None:
   358	        """Clear all stored data across all collections."""
   359	        for col_name in ("agents", "profiles", "messages", "tasks", "schedules", "mail_seeds", "cards"):
   360	            col = self.db.collection(col_name)
   361	            docs = col.stream()
   362	            for doc in docs:
   363	                doc.reference.delete()

    44	class _FakeResponse:
    45	    def __init__(self, status_code: int, payload: dict | None = None, text: str = ""):
    46	        self.status_code = status_code
    47	        self._payload = payload or {}
    48	        self.text = text or str(payload)
    49	
    50	    def json(self):
    51	        return self._payload
    52	
    53	
    54	class _FakeSession:
    55	    """Records calls and returns a canned response or raises a canned
    56	    exception, standing in for requests.Session in client.py's tests."""
    57	
    58	    def __init__(self, response=None, raise_exc=None):
    59	        self._response = response
    60	        self._raise_exc = raise_exc
    61	        self.calls: list[tuple[str, str, dict]] = []
    62	
    63	    def _handle(self, method: str, url: str, **kwargs):
    64	        self.calls.append((method, url, kwargs))
    65	        if self._raise_exc is not None:
    66	            raise self._raise_exc
    67	        return self._response
    68	
    69	    def post(self, url, **kwargs):
    70	        return self._handle("POST", url, **kwargs)
    71	
    72	    def get(self, url, **kwargs):
    73	        return self._handle("GET", url, **kwargs)

    49	class KnowledgeDiscoveryService:
    50	    """Core service coordinating matching, transmission, consent, and audit."""
    51	
    52	    def __init__(
    53	        self,
    54	        store: Store,
    55	        transmission: TransmissionLayer | None = None,
    56	        matching_engine: MatchingEngine | None = None,
    57	    ) -> None:
    58	        self.store = store
    59	        self.transmission = transmission or TransmissionLayer(store)
    60	        self.matching_engine = matching_engine or MatchingEngine()
    61	        # No in-process requester/ask maps (V-2): Cloud Run scales to zero and
    62	        # restarts between requests, so every link lives in message payloads
    63	        # (requester_id / query_id / ask_audit_id) and is derived from the store.
    64	
    65	    def submit_query(self, requester_id: str, question_text: str) -> QuerySubmissionResult:
    66	        """Submit a question, run 2-track matching, and dispatch asks/no_connection records."""
    67	        # 1. Record query message
    68	        query_msg = self.transmission.send(
    69	            from_entity=requester_id,
    70	            to_entity="system",
    71	            intent="query",
    72	            payload_type="query",
    73	            payload={"question_text": question_text, "requester_id": requester_id},
    74	        )
    75	
    76	        # 2. Retrieve registered agents and all employee profiles
    77	        agents = self.store.list_agents(active_only=True)
    78	        profiles = self.store.list_profiles()
    79	
    80	        # 3. Run exploratory matching (2 tracks × 2 stages)
    81	        matching_res = self.matching_engine.run_matching(question_text, agents, profiles)
    82	
    83	        # 4. Record dropped candidates as no_connection in audit log
    84	        for dropped in matching_res.dropped_candidates:
    85	            self.transmission.send(
    86	                from_entity="system",
    87	                to_entity=dropped.agent.agent_id,
    88	                intent="no_connection",
    89	                payload_type="no_connection",
    90	                payload={
    91	                    "reason_text": dropped.reason_text,
    92	                    "cited_item_keys": list(dropped.cited_item_keys),
    93	                    "score": dropped.score,
    94	                },
    95	                candidate_profile=dropped.profile,
    96	            )
    97	
    98	        # 5. Dispatch asks to qualified candidates (up to max k=3)
    99	        dispatched_asks: list[Message] = []
   100	        for qual in matching_res.qualified_candidates:

     7	- Audit trail recording for all dispatched and rejected messages.

    21	    """Exception raised when a transmission fails or is rejected."""
    22	
    23	    def __init__(self, message: Message) -> None:
    24	        super().__init__(f"Transmission rejected: intent={message.intent}, reason={message.payload.get('reason')}")
    25	        self.message = message
    26	
    27	
    28	class TransmissionLayer:
    29	    """Dispatches messages, validates schemas/intents, applies private masks, and logs audits."""
    30	
    31	    def __init__(self, store: Store) -> None:
    32	        self.store = store
    33	
    34	    @staticmethod
    35	    def _reason_leaks_private(profile: Any, reason_text: str) -> bool:
    36	        """Return True if reason_text contains fragments of any private item body.
    37	
    38	        Defense beyond LLM self-report (V-1/S-1): even if the model cites only
    39	        public keys, quoting private content in the reason forces the mask.
    40	        Comparison is whitespace-normalized, case-insensitive, over sliding
    41	        fragments so partial quotes are caught.
    42	        """
    43	        if not reason_text:
    44	            return False
    45	        norm_reason = "".join(reason_text.lower().split())
    46	        for item in profile.items:
    47	            if getattr(item, "visibility", "public") != "private":
    48	                continue
    49	            norm_body = "".join(item.body.lower().split())
    50	            if len(norm_body) < 12:
    51	                if norm_body and norm_body in norm_reason:
    52	                    return True
    53	                continue
    54	            step = 8
    55	            frag_len = 15
    56	            for i in range(0, len(norm_body) - frag_len + 1, step):
    57	                if norm_body[i : i + frag_len] in norm_reason:
    58	                    return True
    59	        return False
    60	
    61	    def _resolve_profile(
    62	        self,
    63	        from_entity: str,
    64	        to_entity: str,
    65	        candidate_profile: Profile | None = None,
    66	    ) -> Profile | None:
    67	        """Resolve candidate profile to check item visibility."""
    68	        if candidate_profile is not None:
    69	            return candidate_profile
    70	
    71	        # Try to resolve to_entity
    72	        agent_to = self.store.get_agent(to_entity)
    73	        if agent_to is not None:
    74	            prof = self.store.get_profile(agent_to.employee_id)
    75	            if prof is not None:
    76	                return prof
    77	
    78	        prof_to = self.store.get_profile(to_entity)
    79	        if prof_to is not None:
    80	            return prof_to
    81	
    82	        # Try to resolve from_entity
    83	        agent_from = self.store.get_agent(from_entity)
    84	        if agent_from is not None:
    85	            prof = self.store.get_profile(agent_from.employee_id)
    86	            if prof is not None:
    87	                return prof

    90	        if prof_from is not None:

codex
新規または修正不十分な欠陥は5件です。C-36〜C-40／Z-1〜Z-6そのものは再掲していません。

1. **W-1 — 種別: 設計／重大度: Critical — consent の本人性確認が複合IDを拘束していない**

   `require_self` は URL の `agent_id` と本人を照合するだけです。しかし consent は本文の `ask_audit_id` も操作対象であり、現行実装は取得した ask の `to_entity == agent_id` を検証せず consent 状態を更新します。[design.md](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/design/knowledge-discovery/design.md:468) [server.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:385) [service.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/service.py:136)

   **破綻シナリオ:** 監査画面で他候補宛ての `audit_id` を知った社員が、自分の `agent_id` をURLに入れ、他候補の `ask_audit_id` を送る。本人照合を通過したまま他人の ask を granted/declined に変更し、偽のマッチ提案を生成できる。二重POSTも pending→decision のCASがないため結果を重複生成できる。

   `original_ask.intent`、`original_ask.to_entity == agent_id`、`consent_state == pending` を同一トランザクションで検証・遷移する条件を、ゴール23へ追加する必要があります。

2. **W-2 — 種別: 設計／重大度: Critical — 権限表が閉じておらず、systemキーが実質的な全テナント閲覧鍵になる**

   「主体×エンドポイント権限表」と称していますが、`/api/me`、`/api/agents`、監査、`/attachments`、`/api/probe` の human/demo/system ごとの許否が表になっていません。特に「横断到達はsystemのsweepのみ」という記述と、systemが任意の `X-KD-Tenant`＋`employee_id` でdigestを取得できる記述は矛盾します。[design.md](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/design/knowledge-discovery/design.md:471) [design.md](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/design/knowledge-discovery/design.md:485) [design.md](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/design/knowledge-discovery/design.md:487)

   **破綻シナリオ:** あるRuntimeまたは運用経路から共通APIキーが漏れると、攻撃者は `X-KD-Tenant` をB社へ変更し、任意社員のdigestを取得できる。DB分離は防壁にならない。humanが同ヘッダを付けた場合の拒否・無視も未規定です。さらに現行 `/api/probe` は監査を書き換える一方、権限表に存在せず、`/attachments` は現在無認証です。[server.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:255) [server.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:501)

   全ルートを行、3主体を列とするdefault-deny表が必要です。`X-KD-Tenant` はsystem以外では拒否し、systemキーを全テナント鍵として受容するなら、その侵害半径を明記してください。より堅くするなら「全テナントsweep鍵」と「テナント限定Runtime鍵」を分けます。

3. **W-3 — 種別: 設計／重大度: High — reconciliationが不完全取得を削除・完了と誤認する**

   取得結果にないTaskをdone、Calendar予定を削除する設計なのに、「全task list・全ページの取得成功後だけreconciliationする」という完全性バリアがありません。[design.md](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/design/knowledge-discovery/design.md:504) Google Tasksは既定20件でページングされ、Calendarも`nextPageToken`を返します。[Google Tasks tasks.list](https://developers.google.com/workspace/tasks/reference/rest/v1/tasks/list)、[Google Calendar events.list](https://developers.google.com/workspace/calendar/api/v3/reference/events/list)

   **破綻シナリオ:** 21件目以降、別task list、または途中エラー後の未取得項目が「消滅」と判定され、Taskはdone→カードresolved、Calendarリマインドは削除される。さらに初回同期で `last_seen_due` が未設定なら、現在dueとの差により最初から `reschedule_count=1` になります。[design.md](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/design/knowledge-discovery/design.md:501)

   全ページ成功時だけabsent-setを反映し、エラー時は破壊的reconciliationを禁止する必要があります。初回は `last_seen_due` を設定するだけで加算しないことも明記すべきです。また「dismissed後にdue変更なら再判定可」は、現行のterminalカード無条件skipでは実現できないため、dismiss時のdueまたはreschedule世代をカードに保持する必要があります。[secretary.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/secretary.py:354)

4. **W-4 — 種別: 設計／重大度: High — IAP検証の可用性・境界テストが未完成**

   `iss`・`aud`・`email`の方向は正しい一方、公開鍵キャッシュと時計ずれが未定義です。`google-auth` の `verify_token` は既定で時計ずれ0秒で、キャッシュ付きtransportを別途構成できます。[google-auth verify_token](https://googleapis.dev/python/google-auth/latest/reference/google.oauth2.id_token.html) IAP公式は`iat`/`exp`に30秒のずれを許容し、鍵キャッシュを推奨しています。[IAP signed headers](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iap/docs/signed-headers-howto)

   **破綻シナリオ:** 各humanリクエストがgstaticへ依存し、一時障害で全認証が401になる。境界時刻の正当なJWTも拒否される。また `IAP_AUDIENCE` 起動検査を無条件実行すると、`AUTH_MODE=demo_key` が未設定audienceのため起動不能となり、「既存テスト不変」と衝突します。

   `clock_skew_in_seconds=30`、Cache-Control対応キャッシュ、鍵更新失敗時の扱い、audience検査は`AUTH_MODE=iap`時のみ、と明記してください。負系テストにもiss不一致、email欠落、未来iat、skew境界を追加すべきです。

5. **W-5 — 種別: 前提／重大度: High — §16.4は1.5〜2日規模ではなく、完了判定も第三者再現可能になっていない**

   現行serverは単一store/serviceを全ルートがclosure参照しており、ContextRouter化は全ルート改修です。[server.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:166) 加えて、現在のモデルには`source`・`last_seen_due`がなく、Storeにはidentities・削除・reconciliation操作もありませんが、`models.py`は変更対象に列挙されていません。[models.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/models.py:318) [store.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/store.py:27) [design.md](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/design/knowledge-discovery/design.md:511)

   **破綻シナリオ:** 認証、全ルート権限化、マルチDB、3外部API、状態移行、暗号テスト、UI、実機確認を同時投入し、反証前に期限切れになる。ContextRouterも素朴に実装するとテナントごとにFirestoreクライアント＋複数Geminiクライアントを永久キャッシュし、`TENANTS_JSON`変更後も旧文脈が再起動まで残ります。さらに「テナントごとにRuntime」なのに各Runtimeが全テナントsweepを呼ぶため、テナント数に比例して全社巡回が重複します。

   2日厳守なら、FR25〜27（完全な権限表、オフラインIAP署名検証、2テナントInMemory＋可能なら第2DB smoke）を残し、FR28〜30は`SourceConnector`＋`SeedConnector`までへ降格するのが妥当です。少なくともGmailは落とすべきです。第2DBと作者ADCは「手動実機ゲート」、JWT・2テナント・フェイクAPIは「クリーン環境CI」に分離してください。

   現在の実測は `.venv` が97件成功・13件skip、`.venv-agent`が同じB段13件をskipなしで成功です。「97＋13」は110個の独立テストではなく2環境のゲートです。また`/attachments`を認証化すると、キーなし200を期待する既存テストは変更必須なので、「既存テスト不変」は「demo/seedの機能結果不変」と言い換える必要があります。[test_server.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/tests/test_server.py:414)
tokens used
215,836
新規または修正不十分な欠陥は5件です。C-36〜C-40／Z-1〜Z-6そのものは再掲していません。

1. **W-1 — 種別: 設計／重大度: Critical — consent の本人性確認が複合IDを拘束していない**

   `require_self` は URL の `agent_id` と本人を照合するだけです。しかし consent は本文の `ask_audit_id` も操作対象であり、現行実装は取得した ask の `to_entity == agent_id` を検証せず consent 状態を更新します。[design.md](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/design/knowledge-discovery/design.md:468) [server.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:385) [service.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/service.py:136)

   **破綻シナリオ:** 監査画面で他候補宛ての `audit_id` を知った社員が、自分の `agent_id` をURLに入れ、他候補の `ask_audit_id` を送る。本人照合を通過したまま他人の ask を granted/declined に変更し、偽のマッチ提案を生成できる。二重POSTも pending→decision のCASがないため結果を重複生成できる。

   `original_ask.intent`、`original_ask.to_entity == agent_id`、`consent_state == pending` を同一トランザクションで検証・遷移する条件を、ゴール23へ追加する必要があります。

2. **W-2 — 種別: 設計／重大度: Critical — 権限表が閉じておらず、systemキーが実質的な全テナント閲覧鍵になる**

   「主体×エンドポイント権限表」と称していますが、`/api/me`、`/api/agents`、監査、`/attachments`、`/api/probe` の human/demo/system ごとの許否が表になっていません。特に「横断到達はsystemのsweepのみ」という記述と、systemが任意の `X-KD-Tenant`＋`employee_id` でdigestを取得できる記述は矛盾します。[design.md](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/design/knowledge-discovery/design.md:471) [design.md](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/design/knowledge-discovery/design.md:485) [design.md](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/design/knowledge-discovery/design.md:487)

   **破綻シナリオ:** あるRuntimeまたは運用経路から共通APIキーが漏れると、攻撃者は `X-KD-Tenant` をB社へ変更し、任意社員のdigestを取得できる。DB分離は防壁にならない。humanが同ヘッダを付けた場合の拒否・無視も未規定です。さらに現行 `/api/probe` は監査を書き換える一方、権限表に存在せず、`/attachments` は現在無認証です。[server.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:255) [server.py](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:501)

   全ルートを行、3主体を列とするdefault-deny表が必要です。`X-KD-Tenant` はsystem以外では拒否し、systemキーを全テナント鍵として受容するなら、その侵害半径を明記してください。より堅くするなら「全テナントsweep鍵」と「テナント限定Runtime鍵」を分けます。

3. **W-3 — 種別: 設計／重大度: High — reconciliationが不完全取得を削除・完了と誤認する**
